<a class="slide-image-link" href="/zh/product-highlight/m/mpd/bc-2600-top-loading-cr2032-lithium-battery-retainer"><img alt="Top Loading CR2032 Lithium Battery Retainer - MPD" class="img-responsive" src="//www.digikey.tw/-/media/Images/Product%20Highlights/M/MPD%20-%20Memory%20Protection%20Devices/Top%20Loading%20CR2032%20Lithium%20Battery%20Retainer/mpd_BC-2600_image.jpg?la=zh-TW&amp;ts=8eeeb776-3b6f-49c8-b772-122f1a75fe60&amp;h=160&amp;w=240&amp;la=zh-TW&amp;hash=676420CF5291A539473B4AD391E295AA"/></a> <div class="content">
<h2 class="title">
<a href="/zh/product-highlight/m/mpd/bc-2600-top-loading-cr2032-lithium-battery-retainer">BC-2600 頂部裝載 CR2032 鋰電池固定器</a>
</h2>
<p><a href="/zh/supplier-centers/m/memory-protection-devices" target="_self">MPD</a> 的 BC-2600 適用於直徑 20 mm 的電池，提供垂直更換功能</p>
<ul>
<li>CR2032 鋰鈕扣電池通常提供 220 mAh 至 240 mAh</li>
<li>MPD 的專利技術，美國專利號碼 10,333,116</li>
<li>手持式醫療裝置，如溫度計和血糖儀</li>
</ul>
<a class="btn primary" href="/zh/product-highlight/m/mpd/bc-2600-top-loading-cr2032-lithium-battery-retainer">瞭解詳情</a>
</div>
</div><div class="slide" cookie-tracking="ref_page_type=HP;ref_page_sub_type=HP;ref_page_id=%7BD998A0F9-B086-43EE-8377-071A844DD0FC%7D;ref_page_event=Supplier%20Promo%20Click;merch_target=%7B531C88DE-8F16-4274-A9D6-96F8524F80FC%7D;merch_placement=%7B456fcc62-5cbf-4a64-88fc-550d66383795%7D;merch_id=%7B751e4ab8-3c6e-4a8b-aa86-04dbe1df6f83%7D" guid="{751e4ab8-3c6e-4a8b-aa86-04dbe1df6f83}">
<a class="slide-image-link" href="/zh/product-highlight/n/newhaven-display/7-inch-ips-display"><img alt="7.0 Inch IPS Display - Newhaven" class="img-responsive" src="//www.digikey.tw/-/media/Images/Product%20Highlights/N/Newhaven%20Display%20Intl/7.0%20Inch%20IPS%20Display/newhaven_7-IPS-TFT_image.jpg?la=zh-TW&amp;ts=69cf4479-d769-4394-9a7c-92830620b525&amp;h=160&amp;w=240&amp;la=zh-TW&amp;hash=98A41ECD0A982A8500B181D6F9E8E223"/></a> <div class="content">
<h2 class="title">
<a href="/zh/product-highlight/n/newhaven-display/7-inch-ips-display">7.0" 平面轉換 (IPS) 顯示器</a>
</h2>
<p><a href="/zh/supplier-centers/n/newhaven-display" target="_self">Newhaven Display</a> 的 7.0" IPS TFT 顯示器提供電容式觸控型或無觸控型，以及 HDMI 和 LVDS 介面選項。Newhaven Display 的 TFT 產品線中增加了四個採用 IPS 技術的顯示器，以解決和改善典型的扭曲向列式 (TN) LCD 面板的缺點。</p>
<a class="btn primary" href="/zh/product-highlight/n/newhaven-display/7-inch-ips-display">瞭解詳情</a>
</div>
</div><div class="slide" cookie-tracking="ref_page_type=HP;ref_page_sub_type=HP;ref_page_id=%7BD998A0F9-B086-43EE-8377-071A844DD0FC%7D;ref_page_event=Supplier%20Promo%20Click;merch_id=%7B54b1fb31-ac99-43ce-afaa-79889c5bae49%7D;merch_placement=%7B456fcc62-5cbf-4a64-88fc-550d66383795%7D;merch_target=%7B531C88DE-8F16-4274-A9D6-96F8524F80FC%7D" guid="{54b1fb31-ac99-43ce-afaa-79889c5bae49}">
<a class="slide-image-link" href="/zh/product-highlight/p/pui-audio/xtreme-series-speakers"><img alt="Xtreme Series Speakers - PUI Audio" class="img-responsive" src="//www.digikey.tw/-/media/Images/Product%20Highlights/P/PUI%20Audio%20Inc/Xtreme%20Series%20Speakers/pui_xtreme-series-speakers_image.jpg?la=zh-TW&amp;ts=d11b692a-5573-4019-8d05-c45cfb088c65&amp;h=160&amp;w=240&amp;la=zh-TW&amp;hash=690872CF6267CD04952C8336E20001EC"/></a> <div class="content">
<h2 class="title">
<a href="/zh/product-highlight/p/pui-audio/xtreme-series-speakers">Xtreme 系列揚聲器</a>
</h2>
<p><a href="/zh/supplier-centers/p/pui-audio" target="_self">PUI Audio</a> 的 Xtreme 系列揚聲器集結數十年的經驗，設計及打造高效能揚聲器，經得起時間的考驗。這些揚聲器為專門用途而製造，提供高效能和耐用性。每個揚聲器都具有一個過大尺寸、雙層 (65 mm 型號為四層) 包銅鋁線聲音線圈，可將運動質量降至最低、提升功率處理能力，以及改善暫態響應。</p>
<a class="btn primary" href="/zh/product-highlight/p/pui-audio/xtreme-series-speakers">瞭解詳情</a>
</div>
</div><div class="slide" cookie-tracking="ref_page_type=HP;ref_page_sub_type=HP;ref_page_id=%7BD998A0F9-B086-43EE-8377-071A844DD0FC%7D;ref_page_event=Supplier%20Promo%20Click;merch_target=%7B531C88DE-8F16-4274-A9D6-96F8524F80FC%7D;merch_placement=%7B456fcc62-5cbf-4a64-88fc-550d66383795%7D;merch_id=%7Ba5e3344a-d8a6-4a4c-bf74-8943b396ad97%7D" guid="{a5e3344a-d8a6-4a4c-bf74-8943b396ad97}">
<a class="slide-image-link" href="/zh/product-highlight/m/microchip-technology/sst26vf-superflash-memory-ics"><img alt="SST26VF SuperFlash Memory ICs - Microchip" class="img-responsive" src="//www.digikey.tw/-/media/Images/Product%20Highlights/M/Microchip%20Corporation/SST26VF%20SuperFlash%20Memory%20ICs/microchip_SST26VF_image.jpg?la=zh-TW&amp;ts=c1ad84ea-6284-43b3-add4-2782648e6d95&amp;h=160&amp;w=240&amp;la=zh-TW&amp;hash=0B766E903500B0338DC0C097617ADB8F"/></a> <div class="content">
<h2 class="title">
<a href="/zh/product-highlight/m/microchip-technology/sst26vf-superflash-memory-ics">SST26VF SuperFlash® 記憶體 IC</a>
</h2>
<p><a href="/zh/supplier-centers/m/microchip-technology" target="_self">Microchip</a> 推出業界首款 NOR 快閃記憶體，採用原廠編程的唯一 EUI-48™ 和 EUI-64™ 識別碼</p>
<ul>
<li>符合成本效益的隨插即用儲存解決方案</li>
<li>本元件無最低訂購要求</li>
<li>x1/x2/x4 SPI 通訊協定和 SQI 通訊協定</li>
</ul>
<a class="btn primary" href="/zh/product-highlight/m/microchip-technology/sst26vf-superflash-memory-ics">瞭解詳情</a>
</div>

<a class="slide-image-link" href="/zh/product-highlight/w/wakefield/ultimiflux-dispensable-gel"><img alt="uTMiFlux Dispensable Gel - Wakefield" class="img-responsive" src="//www.digikey.tw/-/media/Images/Product%20Highlights/W/Wakefield/ulTIMiFlux%20Dispensable%20Gel/wakefield_uITIMiFlux_image.jpg?la=zh-TW&amp;ts=10a8a082-1563-4ac7-9911-a1c217f2fe36&amp;h=160&amp;w=240&amp;la=zh-TW&amp;hash=A4D9CF60D9A8B83E86420505124A30BC"/></a> <div class="content">
<h2 class="title">
<a href="/zh/product-highlight/w/wakefield/ultimiflux-dispensable-gel">ulTIMiFlux™ 高效能熱固化可釋出凝膠</a>
</h2>
<p><a href="/zh/supplier-centers/w/wakefield" target="_self">Wakefield</a>-Vette 的 ulTIMiFlux 完全固化可釋出凝膠，可在不同寬度的間隙處實現低熱阻抗值</p>
<ul>
<li>全固化</li>
<li>低壓力下的高貼合性</li>
<li>單劑釋出</li>
</ul>
